> **The methods and traditions of the warriors are my own. From my father’s meditations on death to the lessons of my tutors in the Dromund Kaas academy, I was nurtured by the dark side and grew strong. Others dull their senses with spice and drink, but warriors know that combat is the only experience to be savored.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **After landing on Ilum, you struck the first blow against the Republic by slaughtering the Duros shocktroopers and their commander, Jedi Master Kaedan. You returned to Grand Moff Regus to find him in a heated discussion with Darth Malgus, a member of the Dark Council. Both men agreed that the next threat to be tackled are the Republic’s Kaleesh mercenaries and their leader, General Gelrex. The best way to draw General Gelrex out would be to challenge him in combat through an ancient Kaleesh ritual.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **Born under the name of Veradun, Darth Malgus grows into a ruthless Sith Lord and one of the key members of their Dark Council. In 3,653 BBY, he is instrumental in the devastating sneak attack on the Jedi Temple and overall plundering of the planet Coruscant, which he fully expects to be pulverized into space dust by an aerial bombardment that never materializes. Disillusioned with both the Empire and human feeling in general, Malgus ultimately kills his longtime lover Daru to purge himself of all emotion.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **To serve on the Dark Council is to achieve the greatest position of honor, power, and influence in the Empire. Many Sith spend their lives plotting their ascension to the council, but with millions vying for only 12 seats, the competition is cutthroat. The powerful few who join the council often serve for only a few months, their lives cut short by internal power struggles or external enemies who target them for assassination. Once every few centuries, a bold few make the foolish mistake of defying the Emperor’s will - a path that inevitably leads to ruin.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): **It is no accident that I took the Darth title when I gained mastery of the dark side, nor is it an accident that Kaan and his followers rejected it. It is a title of power. It carries authority and is crowned by the judgement of history. It symbolizes transformation. When I took Darth as my title, I put away my childhood name. What does it matter that I was once a miner or a soldier? The only thing that matters is what I will achieve.

> **There in the hangar I first encountered the most dangerous embodiment of the dark side I have ever experienced - the man who would later be known as Darth Malgus. Malgus and his master must’ve sensed our presence. They landed in their interceptor in the hangar entrance, blocking our escape, and the two Sith rushed to confront us. I had engaged in hundreds of practice duels with my fellow Padawans, but nothing prepared me for the sheer ferocity of the Sith. Darth Malgus would have killed me then and there were it not for Master Kao.**

> **At an early age, it was evident that there was a dark will behind young Veradun’s sensitivity to the Force. Acknowledging his impressive potential, his adoptive father arranged for Veradun to attend the Sith Academy on Dromund Kass where he would train under Sith Master Vindican. Now, having risen to the status of Sith Lord, dubbed Darth Malgus, he has become a formidable commander in the Imperial Army. Following his vision that he will be responsible for the fall of the Republic and its Jedi Order, Malgus takes his place as a defining force in the Great Galactic War.**

> **In the days of The Old Republic, there was a Sith Lord whose name struck terror in the hearts of any that heard it: Darth Malgus. Raised in the dark side since childroom, Malgus swiftly ascended through the ranks of the Sith, destroying all who stood in his way, eventually declaring himself Emperor. His strength in the dark side was matched only by his confidence. After all, the Force gave him a vision of galactic domination and the destruction of the Jedi by his hand. It was to be his destiny.**

> **Leading the remaining troops up the mountain trail, a tremor in the Force echoed through me. The defenders of Ord Radama had placed mines on the path. Enraged by such cowardice, I hurled a boulder from the mountainside onto the path in front of us. I then slowly rolled it twenty paces ahead of our own progress, using focused mental energy channeled through the dark side. The stone detonated each of the mines and scattered a nest of ambushers. My fire troopers took care of them with the spray of their flamethrower.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **When the Dark Council suffers multiple losses in the war and the Emperor vanishes after battling a lone Jedi Knight on Dromund Kaas, Darth Malgus senses the opportunity to seize supreme authority. Malgus summons his allies to the Emperor’s abandoned space fortress and broadcasts a galaxy-wide message declaring himself leader of a new Empire. To save his people, Malgus enacts a plan to burn the rotten Empire to the ground and forge a new one from the ashes. Malgus’s rule would be predicated on unity and strength through diversity, forming an unbreakable chain to encircle the galaxy.**

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): He’d been sloppy, so lost in his need for revenge that he’d failed to properly evaluate the Jedi’s power. He’d surrendered his reason to bloodlust. - Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): A strong gust of dust and loose debris blew out of the entrance. The explosions on the upper levels began, grew louder, fiercer. Stone cracked. Large chunks fell from the Temple’s façade and crashed to the ground. Flames were visible through the entrance. A whole series of explosions followed in rapid succession, the sound of the snapping spine of the Jedi Order.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:707`

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): The huge edifice, a symbol of the Jedi for centuries, began to fall in on itself. The towers collapsed in its wake, the huge spires crumbling as if in slowed motion. A jet of fire and bits of rock moving faster than the speed of sound exploded out of the now-collapsing entrance.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:709`

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Instead of taking cover, Malgus fell into the Force, raised both of his hands, palms outward, and formed a transparent wall of power before himself and his warriors. His fellow Sith joined him, mirroring his gesture, mirroring his power. Rocks and debris pelted into the shared barrier, the speeding shrapnel of ruin. The jet of flame struck it and parted around it, water to a stone.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:711`

> **Malgus loosed a furious series of overhand strikes that forced the Zabrak to retreat rapidly. > **Malgus augmented his strength with the Force, took a two-handed grip on his blade, and unleashed an overhand slash designed to split the Jedi in half. The Jedi sidestepped the blow and crosscut with his blue blade at Malgus’s throat. Malgus got his blade up in time, parried, and slammed a kick into the Jedi’s mid-section. The blow folded the Jedi in half, sent him reeling backward five paces. Malgus leapt into the air, flipped, landed behind him, and drove his blade through the Jedi. Roaring with battle lust, Malgus sought another opponent.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **Malgus picked up his pace, turned the walk into a charge. His boots thumped off the wood floor of the hall. He did not bother with finesse. He vented his rage in a continuous roar as he unleashed a furious series of blows: an overhand slash that Adraas parried; a low stab that Adraas barely sidestepped; a side kick that connected to Adraas’s side, broke ribs, and flung Adraas fully across the narrow axis of the hall. He crashed into a column and the impact split it as would lightning a tree.**

> [i][size=12][b]Recovered from the wreckage of his coup attempt and revived with a dizzying array of exotic and cutting-edge technologies, Darth Malgus is as powerful and ruthless as ever. His face bears even more scars, his body even more cybernetics; his armor has been reconstructed and enhanced to increase his strength and endurance further still. But through all of this change, Malgus the man has retained the same unyielding determination in pursuit of his sole, brutal goal: the total annihilation of the Jedi, the destruction of the corrupt Republic they serve, and the evolution of the Empire into a society worthy of the power of the Sith.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **Republic leaders have traveled to Alderaan to engage in promised peace talks with the Sith Empire. The most powerful Jedi have accompanied them to safeguard against an Imperial deception. The Empire’s real motive, however, was simply to lure the Republic’s strongest defenders away from Coruscant and set the stage for an audacious attack. Under the command of Lord Angral, the Sith fleet approaches the Republic’s capital planet for the first time in centuries. In advance of the fleet, the strongest Sith Warriors have flown a stolen Republic ship into Coruscant’s orbit. Their mission is critical - to destroy the planet’s defense grid mainframe hidden in the heart of the Jedi Temple.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): **Aryn did not bother to respond. She dwelled in the Force, floated in and on the warm network of lines that connected all things, one to another. Her consciousness expanded to see and feel everything near her. She focused on her perception of the passage of time, first on how it felt as she moved through it, then on spreading it, stretching it, until she could linger in a millisecond as if it were a moment, then a minute. To Zeerid it would appear that she were a blur of motion, existing simultaneously in multiple places. To her, it felt as if the universe around her had stilled. She smiled, seeing the moments that hung before her, each millisecond a long moment in which she could think, in which she could act. The effort taxed her, and she knew she could not maintain it for long.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> **I entered the battlefield and was immediately disheartened to realize that the Sith Lord Darth Malgus was leading the enemy forces. After our last confrontation over Korriban, I had dreaded the possibility of another confrontation with Malgus, and my life was nearly forfeit again on this occasion. It was only the determination and strength of Captain Malcom that prevented it from happening. Together though, we managed to defeat Darth Malgus and left him for dead, buried beneath a mountain of rubble.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

Context: Star Wars: The Old Republic - Hope Cinematic Trailer

> **Malgus’s brutal campaign nearly ended forever when he suffered a crushing defeat battling Jace Malcom, commander of the elite Havoc Squad, and Satele Shan on Alderaan. The Empire’s greatest surgeons and cyberneticists repaired Malgus’s broken body and installed a special breathing apparatus to conceal his ruined face. Malgus emerged from this ordeal lusting for revenge, and he volunteered to lead the devastating surprise attack on the Jedi Temple at Coruscant. Though he had hoped to find Satele Shan among the temple’s defenders, Malgus was content to see the Jedi Temple blasted into rubble along with everyone else inside.**
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Context: [image: https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/users/4212/12/22/43/album/recons10.png]

> **Even before the Great War, Malgus was widely regarded as one of the greatest warriors in the Sith Empire. His patriotism, commitment, and will were unsurpassed by his peers and prized by his pureblooded Sith Master, Lord Vindican. Malgus led multiple expeditions into the Unknown Regions when the Empire’s resources grew scarce or its borders required expansion. He was revered by the Imperial soldiers under his command, who found in Malgus a Sith who respected their service and drove them to excellence.**

Note (our notes, around the quote): Malgus is said by The Old Republic's then-lead writer, Charles Boyd, to be only a "bit" below Arcann in Force power as of Onslaught, but having far more fighting experience and tenacity, so in "a fair fight devoid of narrative," Boyd could see a battle between the two going either way. For context, Arcann was far more powerful than the protagonists, the Hero of Tython, the Barsen'thor, Darth Nox, and the Emperor's Wrath II, to the point where defeating him seemed a hopeless prospect without artificial empowerment from Valkorion.

> **Aside from the Sith Emperor himself, Darth Malgus is easily the most renowned and feared Sith lord in the galaxy. His destruction of the Jedi Temple on Coruscant has become legendary, just one of the many stunning victories won by the Empire's deadliest warrior.**

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: Malgus engages Satele at 0:22, then at 0:25 briefly blocks an oncoming strike from Kao while Vindican distracts Satele, after which Malgus and Satele re-engage at 0:28, fighting until 0:35 when Malgus disarms Satele and is about to land the killing blow. Essentially, Malgus had Satele dead-to-rights in seven seconds, and could have killed her right then and there, a notion corroborated by Satele’s own testimony:
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:1178`

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Only Kao’s lightsaber throw saves Satele; she recovers and takes advantage of the opening created by Kao, kicking Malgus away. After Kao pushes Vindican back, there is a brief lull in combat, during which Satele escapes and Kao is left to fend off the Sith duo by himself. They engage at 1:10, after which the camera cuts away to focus on Satele’s escape, but we cut back to them 1:28 when they’re still fighting. We see that Kao is outclassing both opponents, getting hits in while the Sith aren’t making much progress. Kao manages to separate his opponents and incapacitates Vindican at 1:55 by impaling him. The battle is now just between Malgus and Kao, but the latter is visibly tired while Malgus is still fresh and angry. Kao tries to pummel Malgus by hurling objects at him, but Malgus bulldozes through and clashes blades with him at 2:24. The fight is one-sided and swift, with Kao getting handily overpowered and gutted at 2:33.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: Satele charges at Malgus, clashing blades at 0:02. After a few exchanges, Malgus starts pushing Satele back at 0:06, forcing her into a bladelock at 0:11 that she only breaks out of by exploiting the environment. They disengage and re-engage at 0:22. Satele tries to dance around Malgus but the Sith Lord pushes her with telekinesis, and bisects her lightsaber at 0:31. The fight would have been over had Satele not stopped Malgus’ blade with tutaminis and had Jace Malcom not intervened, something which Satele herself once again confesses:

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): In summary, it’s futile for Satele Shan to engage Darth Malgus head-on as she gets overpowered in ten seconds, and attempting evasive maneuvers around him won’t work either as the Sith is precise and sophisticated enough with his technique to counter it effectively in only a few seconds. All in all, Satele would likely die to Malgus in about 10 to 20 seconds no matter which strategy she decides to employ, assuming no one interrupts the fight.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: For context, this fight happened right after Malgus had suffered tremendous injuries and was clearly not in optimal condition (see the “PHYSICAL AUGMENTATION - DURABILITY” section for details). The fight itself begins by Malgus unloading on the Jedi, not giving him any breathing room. However, Malgus realizes the Jedi is merely feigning weakness and baiting him so that a second Jedi could ambush him. He senses the second Jedi hiding nearby, kicks his current opponent away, and kills the interloper with the Force before he can strike. The first Jedi then charges at Malgus, but gets a faceful of lightning instead, turning him into pretty much a charred corpse. Malgus ends the deed with a stab through the heart.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:1252`

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): In summary, while the Jedi wasn’t giving it his all initially, the fact that he his first instinct was to go for a sneak attack indicates he could not truly have stood up to Darth Malgus in a duel anyway. This is corroborated by how easily he goes down once Malgus brings in the Force, denoting a great power gap, which is most often the deciding factor in lightsaber duels. The same likely applies to the second Jedi, whom Malgus effortlessly crushed with telekinesis.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): A flash of lavender skin drew his gaze—Eleena. She ducked under a saber slash and dived to her side, firing half a dozen blaster shots as she did so. The Padawan who’d tried to kill her, a female Zabrak, the horns of her head gilt with colored pigments, deflected the shots as she closed in for another blow. Eleena flipped to her feet, still firing, but the Padawan deflected every shot and drew nearer.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): The battle turned ever more chaotic. Jedi and Sith leapt, bounded, rolled, and flipped as red lines intersected with those of blue and green. Blasts of power sent bodies flying through the air, against walls, pulled loose rocks from the ceiling and sent them crashing into flesh. The hall was a cacophony of sound: shouts, screams, the hum of lightsabers, the intermittent sound of weapons-fire. Malgus walked in its midst, reveled in it.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): The high-pitched sound of Eleena’s blasters drew his attention. She bounded past him and over the slain female Jedi Knight’s corpse, a lavender blur firing rapidly. Putting her back to a column, she crouched and sought targets for her blasters. She met his eyes, winked, and signaled behind him. He whirled to see a score or more Republic soldiers rushing into the hall from a side room, blaster rifles tracing hot lines through the battlefield. Eleena answered with shots of her own.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Before Malgus could dispatch the soldiers, the Mandalorian rose from somewhere behind them, her jetpack spitting fire, her head-to-toe silver-and-orange armor gleaming in the fire of the hall. - Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: In the cinematic, Malgus appears at 0:05, tossing a Jedi over his head with one hand and immediately slashing the throat of another one with his lightsaber a split second later, then pivoting to strike another Jedi in the head an instant later. - Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): The novelization of the skirmish is a bit different, with Malgus’ first kill being a Jedi with whom he clashes blades once, kicks away, pursues with a Force leap and kills. He then kills a Jedi with a Force push and three more with Force lightning. Afterwards he is assaulted by three Jedi. Malgus dances around them, blitzing one and slamming her to the ground, sending another crashing into a wall with telekinesis, and strangling the third to death with his sheer strength. Another incoming Jedi Malgus simply chokes out with telekinesis without a second glance, and the last one he punches in the stomach and is about to strike down until Ven Zallow intercepts his blade, initiating their duel.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:1318`

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): In summary, Darth Malgus defeated three Jedi in less than two seconds in melee combat and at other points in the fight was stomping them with similar expeditiousness either through martial or Force prowess. No one besides Ven Zallow could last more than a few seconds against him and even whole groups were felled in short order.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: The fight begins very abruptly as Malgus and Zallow happen to cross blades during the Sacking of Coruscant. They lock blades at 0:01 for roughly a second until Malgus’ strength pushes Zallow back. The Sith Lord then unleashes a flurry of vicious strikes which Zallow either parries or evades, but Malgus’ kick catches him in the chest at 0:06 and sends him flying ten meters back. Two lesser Sith attempt to intervene but Zallow swiftly cuts them down only to face Malgus’ lightsaber hurling towards him. The Jedi Master manages to jump out of the way but leaves himself vulnerable for Malgus to push him mid-air through a wall of duracrete. The Sith Lord pursues the Jedi with a massive Force leap but Zallow dodges and charges at Malgus, ducks to evade the sparks the Sith sends at him by dislodging his lightsaber from the floor, and re-engages him blade-to-blade at 0:23. The combatants exchange strikes until at 0:28 Zallow punches Malgus’ respirator mask. Malgus feigns to have been hurt, which prompts Zallow to take advantage of the seeming opening at the risk of leaving himself vulnerable as well. The mistake costs Zallow his life as Malgus impales him through the abdomen.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).
Our notes: `suspect-insight/respect-threads/darth-malgus.md:1366`

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): All in all, the two combatants appeared fairly evenly matched. Ven Zallow displayed greater agility and speed, while Darth Malgus displayed superior strength. Zallow had the durability to take vast amounts of punishment, however, so Malgus ultimately decides to trick his opponent with a feint, rather than attempting to power through.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): To put this feat into context, Zallow was capable of speedblitzing two of the Sith Empire’s greatest Sith Warriors (see the “PHYSICAL AUGMENTATION - SPEED” section for details). While Malgus himself had trouble keeping up with Zallow’s speed, the fact that he could contend as a relative equal overall does speak to the idea that he could decimate the Sith Warriors in question with similar efficiency. Zallow was also noted to be Malgus’ most famous kill, arguably placing him above Kao Cen Darach. By proxy then, Malgus would also be greater than Kao, indicating vast growth as a duelist between Return and Deceived:
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: The duel begins by Malgus jumping down from a shuttle on top of Aryn but she holds her ground under the impact. They exchange strikes and dialogue, utilizing both straightforward as well as environmental ways to undermine the opposition, neither successful. Malgus then unleashes a torrent of lightning at her as she is about to jump down on him but she powers through and martial combat resumes, blade slashes intermixed with unarmed strikes and Force attacks. The duel comes to an abrupt halt as Zeerid swoops in on a speeder and convinces Aryn to abandon the fight.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): In summary, both combatants were virtually perfect equals, with Darth Malgus being somewhat stronger but Aryn Leneer being slightly faster and more agile. No real edge can be afforded to either, although it should be noted that as one of the Jedi participating in the peace talks on Alderaan, Aryn was one of the most powerful Jedi in the Order at the time, and Malgus was able to equal her:

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Breakdown: The fight begins by Adraas demonstrating his newfound power with a burst of lightning, but Malgus casually walks through it and sends it back. The opponents then engage with lightsabers, but Malgus has him on the back foot on the first strike; the second he barely parries; and the third crushes his ribs and sends him crashing into a column. Adraas tries to retaliate but Malgus grips him with telekinesis and slams him onto the ground. Adraas tries to charge again, but Malgus discharges veins of lightning and roasts him completely. Adraas unleashes a final saber assault, but it is effortlessly met and exceeded by Malgus, who quickly severs Adraas’ arm. Malgus then finishes the job by choking Adraas out.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): From the testimonies of the strike team members we can also glean that Malgus was fighting the Hero with his lightsaber, and was blasting the Voidhound with Force attacks, which the latter sidestepped. The Barsen'thor claimed he was stronger than Malgus in the Force, which means the Hero was be too; however, the Hero stated Malgus' lightsaber form was flawless, and since they all confessed they would have lost to him alone, it is likely Malgus was the Hero's superior as a duelist. Near the end, Malgus got desperate and too heated, which indicates he was already losing, and this allowed one of the members to finish the job, although it is unclear who exactly scored the final blow.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Years later, when Malgus has a rematch with one of the protagonists who has now become the Alliance Commander, he believes that he will be able to kill them easily. He doesn't know of the power growth the Commander went through during the previous expansions and believes the death of the Sith Emperor to be nothing but a rumor, as indicated by his amazement following the duel, so he thinks he is fighting them at the same power level as they were during the False Emperor flashpoint. Malgus's confidence should therefore be taken as an indicator of his great superiority towards the Act III protagonists individually as of the False Emperor.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): Yet, seeing as they survived only with minor injuries despite having taken a long fall and being buried under the wreckage, it can be deduced the Commander occupied Malgus's attention for the most part, and he didn't really have the opportunity to stick it to Idair and Peralun. This is corroborated by the positions of the combatants at the start of the ending cutscene: the Commander is closest to Malgus and facing him directly, and Malgus is clutching an abdominal wound, while Idair and Peralun and far behind him. Nonetheless, Malgus pressured the Commander significantly.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> Many of his most important accolades are missing. As is the mindset he entered which Sidious used to find the path to 'true power'. Said mindset afforded him daily power growth for thirteen years before we get to SWTOR, where he's obviously dwarfing himself as of the novel. Then there's years of growth after his stint and near-death.
>
> And which accolades are those?
>
> In Deceived, you’ll learn the story of Darth Malgus and his rise to power. Delve into the dark secrets and history that shaped one of the most powerful Sith Lords the Old Republic would ever know…
>
> The second novel set in the Old Republic era and based on the massively multiplayer online game Star Wars: The Old Republic, ramps up the action and brings readers face-to-face for the first time with a Sith warrior to rival the most sinister of the Order's Dark Lords—Darth Malgus, the mysterious, masked Sith of the wildly popular Deceived and Hope game trailers.
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

> The "no other Sith could have defeated Revan" quote is still made within the context of a **dream team** effort. Malgus saying that they could have never defeated Revan without the dream team indicates that the Wrath, Nox, or even himself likely couldn't have done it alone.
>
> Great work though.  I'll later look to post some other accolade I found along the way as well.
>
> Fair. At face value it seems like a contradiction but since we know it was canonically a group effort, I guess this one overrules the solo options. The same thing happens with the False Emperor fight where the team members say they couldn't have beaten Malgus alone despite that being a possible gameplay option.
>
> Although I recall @ILS  making a case for the solo fights having just as much canonical value as the group fights. Care to chime in?
— Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.

- Note (summary from our notes, not a quotation): BioWare granted solo options to Flashpoints that are story-centric. Freeing Revan from the Maelstrom prison, defeating Revan on the Foundry, stopping Malgus after the Ilum campaign, the raids on Tython and Korriban, etc. The other flashpoints are still designed and operated as group-only content because they are not critical to progressing the story of the game. BioWare has intended for players to be able to play 95% of major story content solo.
  — Source: SI respect-thread directory: Darth Malgus Respect Thread — https://www.suspectinsightforums.com/t66-darth-malgus-respect-thread.
Posted by: not named in our notes (see thread/notes link).

## Darth Marr Respect Thread (Updated 2017) — respect-thread notes

> "Advantages in combat: Augmented lightsaber, customized battle armor, no fear of death."
>
> When The Old Republic was first announced in 2008, the entertainment site [http://www.ign.com IGN] mistakenly reported that the identity of the Sith Emperor was Naga Sadow, though the official website was more vague on the subject. Comments by Daniel Erickson, one of the lead writers for the game, led many to believe that the Emperor had in fact been Emperor during the Great Hyperspace War, though this was later debunked and the Emperor's backstory explained in the 2011 novel The Old Republic: Revan. Written by Drew Karpyshyn, the book revealed the Emperor had been born Tenebrae on the planet Medriaas, and explained both Tenebrae's rise to power as Lord Vitiate and the origins of Vitiate's Empire. The 2012 reference guide Star Wars: The Old Republic Encyclopedia gave further information on the Sith Emperor, revealing that the position of Emperor's Voice and the creation of the Children were results of Revan's attack on the Emperor. It also gave more information on the early Empire, and referred to the Emperor as "Darth Vitiate." The Essential Reader's Companion said that the Emperor was Human, though his original body was that of a pure Sith,
>
> "Notable Sith Warriors: Darth Malgus, Darth Marr, Darth Decimus."
>
> When The Old Republic was first announced in 2008, the entertainment site [http://www.ign.com IGN] mistakenly reported that the identity of the Sith Emperor was Naga Sadow, though the official website was more vague on the subject. Comments by Daniel Erickson, one of the lead writers for the game, led many to believe that the Emperor had in fact been Emperor during the Great Hyperspace War, though this was later debunked and the Emperor's backstory explained in the 2011 novel The Old Republic: Revan. The 2012 reference guide Star Wars: The Old Republic Encyclopedia gave further information on the Sith Emperor, revealing that the position of Emperor's Voice and the creation of the Children were results of Revan's attack on the Emperor. It also gave more information on the early Empire, and referred to the Emperor as "Darth Vitiate." The Essential Reader's Companion said that the Emperor was Human, though his original body was that of a pure Sith,
>
> "Millions strive to become Sith, but many prove too weak to earn the honor. Among the powerful survivors, only the most stalwart and aggressive warriors possess the relentless tenacity to lead the forefront of the Empire's war. Charging into the fray with fearsome battle cries and deadly lightsabers, Sith warriors break their enemies with sheer dominating will and crush any foe who doesn't flee from their unforgiving march. The most triumphant warriors inspire legions of allies with fear and reverence. Under Sith command, bolstered armies fight with roused passion and pride, often decimating superior numbers with their indomitable assaults. Whether striking for the Empire's glory or for personal renown, there is no more dominating force than that of the Sith warrior."
>
> When The Old Republic was first announced in 2008, the entertainment site [http://www.ign.com IGN] mistakenly reported that the identity of the Sith Emperor was Naga Sadow, though the official website was more vague on the subject. What it shows (our notes): Specifically, Revan resisted Emperor Vitiate's probing into learning his secrets --
> Yet the Emperor wanted more than to leach off his fallen adversary’s power to sustain his own twisted existence. Revan could feel the enemy inside his head. He could sense the unmistakable darkness of the Emperor sifting through his thoughts and memories, seeking, probing, digging for answers.
>
> He wanted information on the Republic and the Jedi. How strong were they? Where were they vulnerable? How much did they know about the Sith and the Emperor himself? He wanted information on Revan. What had happened during his own invasion of the Republic? Why had it failed? How had he freed himself from the Emperor’s control?
>
> The answers were all there, but Revan would not surrender them easily. Though he was physically helpless, mentally he was strong enough to wage war against the Emperor, guarding his secrets for however long it might take.
>
> "The Emperor sought to pry the Foundry's location from the Jedi's mind. But for centuries he resisted."
>
> "The Emperor had three hundred years to break this man, and he never succumbed?"

Context: (F) Revan held his own against an Imperial strike team of the Empire's greatest champions until he was killed. The circumstances of the defeat can largely be attributed to suffering three centuries of torture and being drained by Emperor Vitiate and the Dread Masters yet only having days to recover, and that Revan's mind had begun to splinter into two separate, rival entities, interfering with his capacity to fight since both personalities had different intentions within his body. Despite this weakened state, Revan became far more powerful than any of his opponents or any Jedi before him by channeling both sides of the Force in battle.
